## Post-mortem

29 loss(es) and 41 won control(s) examined with the same rule.

| verdict | losses | wins (control) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| REASONING_WRONG | 0 | 0 |
| INFORMATION_MISSING | 0 | 0 |
| VARIANCE | 29 | 41 |

Read the two columns together. A verdict class that appears at the same rate among wins as among losses is describing the games we bet, not the bets we lost.
WARNING: every one of the 70 settled decisions examined landed in the same class (VARIANCE). This classifier separated NOTHING here -- do not read the column of losses as evidence about the reasoning.
Every loss examined came from a decision that recorded NO mechanism check, so not one of them could have been classed REASONING_WRONG by any game. The gap this exposes is in what the systems write down at decision time, not in any parameter.
16 pivot(s) were measured on the run-margin PROXY, not a win probability.

### What this suggests researching
- No signature cleared the pattern floor (>= 3 losses, >= 25% of losses, >= 2x the rate in the won control). These losses look like individual games, not a family.

A post-mortem is a description of what happened, never evidence for a strategy change (docs/RESEARCH_CATALOGUE.md T8: no rescue by threshold change).

### LOSS: market_derived_consensus_h2h_1st_5_innings_home / h2h_1st_5_innings home (game 824717)

Thesis: MARKET_DERIVED, zero-parameter: p_model is the board's own de-vigged consensus for this selection, republished under the identity map by analyze()'s PROJECT phase -- no recalibration intercept, no Platt, no isotonic refit (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md §2). Not a strategy: edge_bps is structurally None; this may be published as prediction confidence only.

**What happened.**
SEA 8 at BOS 3; 81 plate appearances, 0 lead change(s).
Stopped being in doubt in the top of the 3rd -- MLB win probability: the last play after which the winner's own win probability never fell below 50% again.

**The pivot.**
top 3rd, runners on 1B and 2B, 2 out: Josh Naylor singles on a fly ball to left fielder Jarren Duran. Dominic Canzone scores. Julio Rodríguez scores. Josh Naylor to 2nd. Throwing error by left fielder Jarren Duran. Our side: run margin (PROXY, not a probability) -2 -> -4 (-2 runs).
Worst half-inning: top 3rd, 2 RBI -- run margin (PROXY) +0 -> -4 (-4 runs).
Metric: run_margin_proxy -- PROXY: settlement rule 'h2h_1st_5' does not settle on the full-game winner MLB's win-probability series predicts, so the pivot is measured on the run margin instead.
Pitcher on the mound was the starter.

**Verdict: VARIANCE (no_falsifiable_mechanism)**
- no mechanism checks were recorded on this decision, so no part of its thesis could be refuted by any game
- settlement's own thesis_outcome: UNTESTED

**What this post-mortem could not determine.**
- the pivot is measured on a run-margin PROXY, not a win probability -- it can say how many runs the game moved against this bet, never by how much the chance of winning it changed
- this decision recorded no mechanism check, so this post-mortem cannot distinguish 'the reasoning held' from 'the reasoning was never testable' -- the VARIANCE verdict here is a statement about the thesis, not a defence of it
